File: account-service/Dockerfile
================
1: FROM java:8-jre
2: MAINTAINER Alexander Lukyanchikov <sqshq@sqshq.com>
3: 
4: ADD ./target/account-service.jar /app/
5: CMD ["java", "-Xmx200m", "-jar", "/app/account-service.jar"]
6: 
7: EXPOSE 6000

File: account-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/account/AccountApplication.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.account;
 2: import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
 3: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
 4: import org.springframework.cloud.client.circuitbreaker.EnableCircuitBreaker;
 5: import org.springframework.cloud.client.discovery.EnableDiscoveryClient;
 6: import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.EnableFeignClients;
 7: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.method.configuration.EnableGlobalMethodSecurity;
 8: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.EnableOAuth2Client;
 9: @SpringBootApplication
10: @EnableDiscoveryClient
11: @EnableOAuth2Client
12: @EnableFeignClients
13: @EnableCircuitBreaker
14: @EnableGlobalMethodSecurity(prePostEnabled = true)
15: public class AccountApplication {
16: 	public static void main(String[] args) {
17: 		SpringApplication.run(AccountApplication.class, args);
18: 	}
19: }

================
File: account-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/account/client/AuthServiceClient.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.account.client;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.account.domain.User;
 3: import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
 4: import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
 5: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
 6: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMethod;
 7: @FeignClient(name = "auth-service")
 8: public interface AuthServiceClient {
 9: 	@RequestMapping(method = RequestMethod.POST, value = "/uaa/users", consumes = MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_UTF8_VALUE)
10: 	void createUser(User user);
11: }

================
File: account-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/account/client/StatisticsServiceClient.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.account.client;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.account.domain.Account;
 3: import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
 4: import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
 5: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
 6: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMethod;
 8: @FeignClient(name = "statistics-service", fallback = StatisticsServiceClientFallback.class)
 9: public interface StatisticsServiceClient {
10: 	@RequestMapping(method = RequestMethod.PUT, value = "/statistics/{accountName}", consumes = MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_UTF8_VALUE)
11: 	void updateStatistics(@PathVariable("accountName") String accountName, Account account);
12: }

File: account-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/account/config/ResourceServerConfig.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.account.config;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.account.service.security.CustomUserInfoTokenServices;
 3: import feign.RequestInterceptor;
 4: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 5: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.security.oauth2.resource.ResourceServerProperties;
 6: import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
 7: import org.springframework.cloud.security.oauth2.client.feign.OAuth2FeignRequestInterceptor;
 8: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 9: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
10: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
11: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.DefaultOAuth2ClientContext;
12: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.OAuth2RestTemplate;
13: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.token.grant.client.ClientCredentialsResourceDetails;
14: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.EnableResourceServer;
15: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.ResourceServerConfigurerAdapter;
16: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.token.ResourceServerTokenServices;
17: @Configuration
18: @EnableResourceServer
19: public class ResourceServerConfig extends ResourceServerConfigurerAdapter {
20:     private final ResourceServerProperties sso;
21:     @Autowired
22:     public ResourceServerConfig(ResourceServerProperties sso) {
23:         this.sso = sso;
24:     }
25:     @Bean
26:     @ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "security.oauth2.client")
27:     public ClientCredentialsResourceDetails clientCredentialsResourceDetails() {
28:         return new ClientCredentialsResourceDetails();
29:     }
30:     @Bean
31:     public RequestInterceptor oauth2FeignRequestInterceptor(){
32:         return new OAuth2FeignRequestInterceptor(new DefaultOAuth2ClientContext(), clientCredentialsResourceDetails());
33:     }
34:     @Bean
35:     public OAuth2RestTemplate clientCredentialsRestTemplate() {
36:         return new OAuth2RestTemplate(clientCredentialsResourceDetails());
37:     }
38:     @Bean
39:     public ResourceServerTokenServices tokenServices() {
40:         return new CustomUserInfoTokenServices(sso.getUserInfoUri(), sso.getClientId());
41:     }
42:     @Override
43:     public void configure(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {
44:         http.authorizeRequests()
45:                 .antMatchers("/" , "/demo").permitAll()
46:                 .anyRequest().authenticated();
47:     }
48: }

File: account-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/account/controller/AccountController.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.account.controller;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.account.domain.Account;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.account.domain.User;
 4: import com.piggymetrics.account.service.AccountService;
 5: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 6: import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
 8: import javax.validation.Valid;
 9: import java.security.Principal;
10: @RestController
11: public class AccountController {
12: 	@Autowired
13: 	private AccountService accountService;
14: 	@PreAuthorize("#oauth2.hasScope('server') or #name.equals('demo')")
15: 	@RequestMapping(path = "/{name}", method = RequestMethod.GET)
16: 	public Account getAccountByName(@PathVariable String name) {
17: 		return accountService.findByName(name);
18: 	}
19: 	@RequestMapping(path = "/current", method = RequestMethod.GET)
20: 	public Account getCurrentAccount(Principal principal) {
21: 		return accountService.findByName(principal.getName());
22: 	}
23: 	@RequestMapping(path = "/current", method = RequestMethod.PUT)
24: 	public void saveCurrentAccount(Principal principal, @Valid @RequestBody Account account) {
25: 		accountService.saveChanges(principal.getName(), account);
26: 	}
27: 	@RequestMapping(path = "/", method = RequestMethod.POST)
28: 	public Account createNewAccount(@Valid @RequestBody User user) {
29: 		return accountService.create(user);
30: 	}
31: }

================
File: account-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/account/service/AccountServiceImpl.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.account.service;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.account.client.AuthServiceClient;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.account.client.StatisticsServiceClient;
 4: import com.piggymetrics.account.domain.Account;
 5: import com.piggymetrics.account.domain.Currency;
 6: import com.piggymetrics.account.domain.Saving;
 7: import com.piggymetrics.account.domain.User;
 8: import com.piggymetrics.account.repository.AccountRepository;
 9: import org.slf4j.Logger;
10: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
11: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
12: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
13: import org.springframework.util.Assert;
14: import java.math.BigDecimal;
15: import java.util.Date;
16: @Service
17: public class AccountServiceImpl implements AccountService {
18: 	private final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
19: 	@Autowired
20: 	private StatisticsServiceClient statisticsClient;
21: 	@Autowired
22: 	private AuthServiceClient authClient;
23: 	@Autowired
24: 	private AccountRepository repository;
25: 	@Override
26: 	public Account findByName(String accountName) {
27: 		Assert.hasLength(accountName);
28: 		return repository.findByName(accountName);
29: 	}
30: 	@Override
31: 	public Account create(User user) {
32: 		Account existing = repository.findByName(user.getUsername());
33: 		Assert.isNull(existing, "account already exists: " + user.getUsername());
34: 		authClient.createUser(user);
35: 		Saving saving = new Saving();
36: 		saving.setAmount(new BigDecimal(0));
37: 		saving.setCurrency(Currency.getDefault());
38: 		saving.setInterest(new BigDecimal(0));
39: 		saving.setDeposit(false);
40: 		saving.setCapitalization(false);
41: 		Account account = new Account();
42: 		account.setName(user.getUsername());
43: 		account.setLastSeen(new Date());
44: 		account.setSaving(saving);
45: 		repository.save(account);
46: 		log.info("new account has been created: " + account.getName());
47: 		return account;
48: 	}
49: 	@Override
50: 	public void saveChanges(String name, Account update) {
51: 		Account account = repository.findByName(name);
52: 		Assert.notNull(account, "can't find account with name " + name);
53: 		account.setIncomes(update.getIncomes());
54: 		account.setExpenses(update.getExpenses());
55: 		account.setSaving(update.getSaving());
56: 		account.setNote(update.getNote());
57: 		account.setLastSeen(new Date());
58: 		repository.save(account);
59: 		log.debug("account {} changes has been saved", name);
60: 		statisticsClient.updateStatistics(name, account);
61: 	}
62: }

================
File: account-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/account/service/security/CustomUserInfoTokenServices.java
================
  1: package com.piggymetrics.account.service.security;
  2: import org.apache.commons.logging.Log;
  3: import org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;
  4: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.security.oauth2.resource.AuthoritiesExtractor;
  5: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.security.oauth2.resource.FixedAuthoritiesExtractor;
  6: import org.springframework.security.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken;
  7: import org.springframework.security.core.AuthenticationException;
  8: import org.springframework.security.core.GrantedAuthority;
  9: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.OAuth2RestOperations;
 10: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.OAuth2RestTemplate;
 11: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.resource.BaseOAuth2ProtectedResourceDetails;
 12: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.common.DefaultOAuth2AccessToken;
 13: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.common.OAuth2AccessToken;
 14: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.common.exceptions.InvalidTokenException;
 15: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.OAuth2Authentication;
 16: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.OAuth2Request;
 17: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.token.ResourceServerTokenServices;
 18: import java.util.*;
 19: public class CustomUserInfoTokenServices implements ResourceServerTokenServices {
 20: 	protected final Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(getClass());
 21: 	private static final String[] PRINCIPAL_KEYS = new String[] { "user", "username",
 22: 			"userid", "user_id", "login", "id", "name" };
 23: 	private final String userInfoEndpointUrl;
 24: 	private final String clientId;
 25: 	private OAuth2RestOperations restTemplate;
 26: 	private String tokenType = DefaultOAuth2AccessToken.BEARER_TYPE;
 27: 	private AuthoritiesExtractor authoritiesExtractor = new FixedAuthoritiesExtractor();
 28: 	public CustomUserInfoTokenServices(String userInfoEndpointUrl, String clientId) {
 29: 		this.userInfoEndpointUrl = userInfoEndpointUrl;
 30: 		this.clientId = clientId;
 31: 	}
 32: 	public void setTokenType(String tokenType) {
 33: 		this.tokenType = tokenType;
 34: 	}
 35: 	public void setRestTemplate(OAuth2RestOperations restTemplate) {
 36: 		this.restTemplate = restTemplate;
 37: 	}
 38: 	public void setAuthoritiesExtractor(AuthoritiesExtractor authoritiesExtractor) {
 39: 		this.authoritiesExtractor = authoritiesExtractor;
 40: 	}
 41: 	@Override
 42: 	public OAuth2Authentication loadAuthentication(String accessToken)
 43: 			throws AuthenticationException, InvalidTokenException {
 44: 		Map<String, Object> map = getMap(this.userInfoEndpointUrl, accessToken);
 45: 		if (map.containsKey("error")) {
 46: 			this.logger.debug("userinfo returned error: " + map.get("error"));
 47: 			throw new InvalidTokenException(accessToken);
 48: 		}
 49: 		return extractAuthentication(map);
 50: 	}
 51: 	private OAuth2Authentication extractAuthentication(Map<String, Object> map) {
 52: 		Object principal = getPrincipal(map);
 53: 		OAuth2Request request = getRequest(map);
 54: 		List<GrantedAuthority> authorities = this.authoritiesExtractor
 55: 				.extractAuthorities(map);
 56: 		UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken token = new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
 57: 				principal, "N/A", authorities);
 58: 		token.setDetails(map);
 59: 		return new OAuth2Authentication(request, token);
 60: 	}
 61: 	private Object getPrincipal(Map<String, Object> map) {
 62: 		for (String key : PRINCIPAL_KEYS) {
 63: 			if (map.containsKey(key)) {
 64: 				return map.get(key);
 65: 			}
 66: 		}
 67: 		return "unknown";
 68: 	}
 69: 	@SuppressWarnings({ "unchecked" })
 70: 	private OAuth2Request getRequest(Map<String, Object> map) {
 71: 		Map<String, Object> request = (Map<String, Object>) map.get("oauth2Request");
 72: 		String clientId = (String) request.get("clientId");
 73: 		Set<String> scope = new LinkedHashSet<>(request.containsKey("scope") ?
 74: 				(Collection<String>) request.get("scope") : Collections.<String>emptySet());
 75: 		return new OAuth2Request(null, clientId, null, true, new HashSet<>(scope),
 76: 				null, null, null, null);
 77: 	}
 78: 	@Override
 79: 	public OAuth2AccessToken readAccessToken(String accessToken) {
 80: 		throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Not supported: read access token");
 81: 	}
 82: 	@SuppressWarnings({ "unchecked" })
 83: 	private Map<String, Object> getMap(String path, String accessToken) {
 84: 		this.logger.debug("Getting user info from: " + path);
 85: 		try {
 86: 			OAuth2RestOperations restTemplate = this.restTemplate;
 87: 			if (restTemplate == null) {
 88: 				BaseOAuth2ProtectedResourceDetails resource = new BaseOAuth2ProtectedResourceDetails();
 89: 				resource.setClientId(this.clientId);
 90: 				restTemplate = new OAuth2RestTemplate(resource);
 91: 			}
 92: 			OAuth2AccessToken existingToken = restTemplate.getOAuth2ClientContext()
 93: 					.getAccessToken();
 94: 			if (existingToken == null || !accessToken.equals(existingToken.getValue())) {
 95: 				DefaultOAuth2AccessToken token = new DefaultOAuth2AccessToken(
 96: 						accessToken);
 97: 				token.setTokenType(this.tokenType);
 98: 				restTemplate.getOAuth2ClientContext().setAccessToken(token);
 99: 			}
100: 			return restTemplate.getForEntity(path, Map.class).getBody();
101: 		}
102: 		catch (Exception ex) {
103: 			this.logger.info("Could not fetch user details: " + ex.getClass() + ", "
104: 					+ ex.getMessage());
105: 			return Collections.<String, Object>singletonMap("error",
106: 					"Could not fetch user details");
107: 		}
108: 	}
109: }

================
File: auth-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/auth/config/OAuth2AuthorizationConfig.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.auth.config;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.auth.service.security.MongoUserDetailsService;
 3: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 4: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
 5: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 6: import org.springframework.core.env.Environment;
 7: import org.springframework.security.authentication.AuthenticationManager;
 8: import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.NoOpPasswordEncoder;
 9: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.configurers.ClientDetailsServiceConfigurer;
10: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.AuthorizationServerConfigurerAdapter;
11: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.EnableAuthorizationServer;
12: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configurers.AuthorizationServerEndpointsConfigurer;
13: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configurers.AuthorizationServerSecurityConfigurer;
14: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.token.TokenStore;
15: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.token.store.InMemoryTokenStore;
16: @Configuration
17: @EnableAuthorizationServer
18: public class OAuth2AuthorizationConfig extends AuthorizationServerConfigurerAdapter {
19:     private TokenStore tokenStore = new InMemoryTokenStore();
20:     private final String NOOP_PASSWORD_ENCODE = "{noop}";
21:     @Autowired
22:     @Qualifier("authenticationManagerBean")
23:     private AuthenticationManager authenticationManager;
24:     @Autowired
25:     private MongoUserDetailsService userDetailsService;
26:     @Autowired
27:     private Environment env;
28:     @Override
29:     public void configure(ClientDetailsServiceConfigurer clients) throws Exception {
30:         clients.inMemory()
31:                 .withClient("browser")
32:                 .authorizedGrantTypes("refresh_token", "password")
33:                 .scopes("ui")
34:                 .and()
35:                 .withClient("account-service")
36:                 .secret(env.getProperty("ACCOUNT_SERVICE_PASSWORD"))
37:                 .authorizedGrantTypes("client_credentials", "refresh_token")
38:                 .scopes("server")
39:                 .and()
40:                 .withClient("statistics-service")
41:                 .secret(env.getProperty("STATISTICS_SERVICE_PASSWORD"))
42:                 .authorizedGrantTypes("client_credentials", "refresh_token")
43:                 .scopes("server")
44:                 .and()
45:                 .withClient("notification-service")
46:                 .secret(env.getProperty("NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_PASSWORD"))
47:                 .authorizedGrantTypes("client_credentials", "refresh_token")
48:                 .scopes("server");
49:     }
50:     @Override
51:     public void configure(AuthorizationServerEndpointsConfigurer endpoints) throws Exception {
52:         endpoints
53:                 .tokenStore(tokenStore)
54:                 .authenticationManager(authenticationManager)
55:                 .userDetailsService(userDetailsService);
56:     }
57:     @Override
58:     public void configure(AuthorizationServerSecurityConfigurer oauthServer) throws Exception {
59:         oauthServer
60:                 .tokenKeyAccess("permitAll()")
61:                 .checkTokenAccess("isAuthenticated()")
62:                 .passwordEncoder(NoOpPasswordEncoder.getInstance());
63:     }
64: }

================
File: auth-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/auth/config/WebSecurityConfig.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.auth.config;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.auth.service.security.MongoUserDetailsService;
 3: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 4: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 5: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 6: import org.springframework.security.authentication.AuthenticationManager;
 7: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.authentication.builders.AuthenticationManagerBuilder;
 8: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
 9: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.configuration.WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter;
10: import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;
11: @Configuration
12: public class WebSecurityConfig extends WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter {
13:     @Autowired
14:     private MongoUserDetailsService userDetailsService;
15:     @Override
16:     protected void configure(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {
17:         http
18:                 .authorizeRequests().anyRequest().authenticated()
19:                 .and()
20:                 .csrf().disable();
21:     }
22:     @Override
23:     protected void configure(AuthenticationManagerBuilder auth) throws Exception {
24:         auth.userDetailsService(userDetailsService)
25:                 .passwordEncoder(new BCryptPasswordEncoder());
26:     }
27:     @Override
28:     @Bean
29:     public AuthenticationManager authenticationManagerBean() throws Exception {
30:         return super.authenticationManagerBean();
31:     }
32: }

 5: import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
 6: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;

11: import java.security.Principal;
12: @RestController
13: @RequestMapping("/users")
14: public class UserController {
15: 	@Autowired
16: 	private UserService userService;
17: 	@RequestMapping(value = "/current", method = RequestMethod.GET)
18: 	public Principal getUser(Principal principal) {
19: 		return principal;
20: 	}
21: 	@PreAuthorize("#oauth2.hasScope('server')")
22: 	@RequestMapping(method = RequestMethod.POST)
23: 	public void createUser(@Valid @RequestBody User user) {
24: 		userService.create(user);
25: 	}
26: }

36: 		return true;

40: 		return true;
41: 	}

45: 	}

File: auth-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/auth/service/security/MongoUserDetailsService.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.auth.service.security;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.auth.repository.UserRepository;
 3: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 4: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetails;
 5: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetailsService;
 6: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UsernameNotFoundException;
 7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 8: @Service
 9: public class MongoUserDetailsService implements UserDetailsService {
10: 	@Autowired
11: 	private UserRepository repository;
12: 	@Override
13: 	public UserDetails loadUserByUsername(String username) throws UsernameNotFoundException {
14: 		return repository.findById(username).orElseThrow(()->new UsernameNotFoundException(username));
15: 	}
16: }

File: auth-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/auth/service/UserService.java
================
1: package com.piggymetrics.auth.service;
2: import com.piggymetrics.auth.domain.User;
3: public interface UserService {
4: 	void create(User user);
5: }

================
File: auth-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/auth/service/UserServiceImpl.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.auth.service;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.auth.domain.User;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.auth.repository.UserRepository;
 4: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 5: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;
 8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 9: import org.springframework.util.Assert;
10: import java.util.Optional;
11: @Service
12: public class UserServiceImpl implements UserService {
13: 	private final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
14: 	private static final BCryptPasswordEncoder encoder = new BCryptPasswordEncoder();
15: 	@Autowired
16: 	private UserRepository repository;
17: 	@Override
18: 	public void create(User user) {
19: 		Optional<User> existing = repository.findById(user.getUsername());
20: 		existing.ifPresent(it-> {throw new IllegalArgumentException("user already exists: " + it.getUsername());});
21: 		String hash = encoder.encode(user.getPassword());
22: 		user.setPassword(hash);
23: 		repository.save(user);
24: 		log.info("new user has been created: {}", user.getUsername());
25: 	}
26: }

2: MAINTAINER Alexander Lukyanchikov <sqshq@sqshq.com>
3:

6: 
7: EXPOSE 4000

 59: function getConverted(column) {
 60: 	var firstitem, seconditem;
 61: 	for (var key in column) {
 62: 		switch (column[key].currency) {
 63: 			case "RUB": column[key].converted = column[key].amount;
 64: 				break;
 65: 			case "EUR": column[key].converted = (column[key].amount * global.eur).toFixed(3);
 66: 				break;
 67: 			case "USD": column[key].converted = (column[key].amount * global.usd).toFixed(3);
 68: 				break;
 69: 		}
 70: 		switch (column[key].period) {
 71: 			case "MONTH": break;
 72: 			case "HOUR": column[key].converted = (column[key].converted * 730).toFixed(3);
 73: 				break;
 74: 			case "DAY": column[key].converted = (column[key].converted * 30.41666667).toFixed(3);
 75: 				break;
 76: 			case "QUARTER": column[key].converted = (column[key].converted / 3).toFixed(3);
 77: 				break;
 78: 			case "YEAR": column[key].converted = (column[key].converted / 12).toFixed(3);
 79: 				break;
 80: 		}
 81: 		switch (user.checkedCurr) {
 82: 			case "RUB": break;
 83: 			case "EUR": column[key].converted = (column[key].converted / global.eur).toFixed(3);
 84: 				break;
 85: 			case "USD": column[key].converted = (column[key].converted / global.usd).toFixed(3);
 86: 				break;
 87: 		}
 88: 		if (column == incomes) {
 89: 			if (firstitem == undefined) {firstitem = key; $("#incomeslider").data("firstitem", key); }
 90: 			incomesSumMonth += Math.round(column[key].converted);
 91: 			$("#circle-select-1, #circle-select-2, #circle-select-3").append('<option value="' + parseInt(column[key].income_id, 10) + '">' + escape(column[key].title) + '</option>');
 92: 		}
 93: 		else {
 94: 			if (firstitem == undefined) {firstitem = key; $("#expenseslider").data("firstitem", key); }
 95: 			else if (seconditem == undefined) {seconditem = key; $("#expenseslider").data("seconditem", key); }
 96: 			expensesSumMonth += Math.round(column[key].converted);
 97: 			$("#circle-select-1, #circle-select-2, #circle-select-3").append('<option value="' + (parseInt(column[key].expense_id, 10) + 100) + '">' + escape(column[key].title) + '</option>');
 98: 		}
 99: 	}
100: 	if ( Math.abs(incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth) < 10 ) {
101: 		expensesSumMonth = incomesSumMonth;
102: 	}
103: 	$('select').trigger('refresh');
104: }
105: function initStatisticPage() {
106: 	changeCurrency = function () {
107: 		switch (user.checkedCurr) {
108: 			case "RUB":
109: 				if (user.lastCurr == "RUB") { break; }
110: 				else if (user.lastCurr == "USD") { savings.freeMoney = (savings.freeMoney * global.usd).toFixed(3); }
111: 				else if (user.lastCurr == "EUR") { savings.freeMoney = (savings.freeMoney * global.eur).toFixed(3); }
112: 				break;
113: 			case "EUR":
114: 				if (user.lastCurr == "EUR") { break; }
115: 				else if (user.lastCurr == "USD") { savings.freeMoney = (savings.freeMoney * global.usd / global.eur).toFixed(3); }
116: 				else if (user.lastCurr == "RUB") { savings.freeMoney = (savings.freeMoney / global.eur).toFixed(3); }
117: 				break;
118: 			case "USD":
119: 				if (user.lastCurr == "USD") { break; }
120: 				else if (user.lastCurr == "EUR") { savings.freeMoney = (savings.freeMoney * global.eur / global.usd).toFixed(3); }
121: 				else if (user.lastCurr == "RUB") { savings.freeMoney = (savings.freeMoney / global.usd).toFixed(3); }
122: 				break;
123: 		}
124: 		user.lastCurr = user.checkedCurr;
125: 	};
126: 	changeCurrency();
127: 	initCircle = function (whichcircle, item, beforevalue) {
128: 		var sum, value, circle;

401: function separateNumber(val) {
402: 	if (Math.abs (val) > 999) {
403: 		val = val.toString().replace(/(\d)(?=(\d\d\d)+(?!\d))/g, '$1 <span class="lightcircletitle">');
404: 	}
405: 	else {
406: 		val = val.toString().replace(/(\.)/g, '<span class="lightcircletitle">$1');
407: 	}
408: 	return val;
409: };
410: function animatecircle(beforevalue, aftervalue, sum, title) {
411: 	var before = (100 * beforevalue/sum),
412: 			after = (100 * aftervalue/sum),
413: 			R = $(this).data("width") / 2,
414: 			alpha = (after * 0.02 * Math.PI),
415: 			n = 10,
416: 			id = $(this).attr("id"),
417: 			$this = $(this),
418: 			ycord, xcord;
419: 	(function() {
420: 		var fontpx;
421: 		if (id == "outer-circle") {
422: 			if (sum-aftervalue < 999999) { fontpx = 48 }
423: 			else if (sum-aftervalue < 9999999) {fontpx = 37 }
424: 			else { fontpx = 22 }
425: 		}
426: 		else {
427: 			if (aftervalue < 99999) {fontpx = 31}
428: 			else if (aftervalue < 999999) {	fontpx = 27 }
429: 			else { fontpx = 18 }
430: 		}
431: 		$("#" + id + "-value").css({"font-size": fontpx + "px"})
432: 	})();
433: 	if (after <= 25 ) {
434: 		ycord = Math.round( R + (n / 2) - (R * Math.sin(1.57 - alpha)) );
435: 		xcord = Math.round( R + (n) + (R * Math.cos(1.57 - alpha)) );
436: 		animatetime = 500;
437: 	}
438: 	else if (after <= 50 ) {
439: 		ycord = Math.round( R + (n / 2) + (R * Math.sin(alpha - 1.57)) );
440: 		xcord = Math.round( R + (n * 2) + (R * Math.cos(alpha - 1.57)) );
441: 		animatetime = 1400;
442: 	}
443: 	else if (after <= 75 ) {
444: 		ycord = Math.round( R - (n * 2) + (R * Math.sin(alpha - 1.57)) );
445: 		xcord = Math.round( R - (n * 4) + (R * Math.cos(alpha - 1.57)) );
446: 		animatetime = 1500;
447: 	}
448: 	else {
449: 		ycord = Math.round( R + (- n * 3) - (R * Math.sin(1.57 - alpha)) );
450: 		xcord = Math.round( R + (- n * 3) + (R * Math.cos(1.57 - alpha)) );
451: 		animatetime = 1600;
452: 	}
453: 	$("#" + id + "-percent").hide();
454: 	$("#" + id + "-percent").empty().append('<span class="lightcircletitle">' + Math.round(after) + '%</span>');
455: 	setTimeout(function () { $("#" + id + "-percent").css({"left": xcord, "top": ycord}).fadeIn(400)}, animatetime-200);
456: 	$({ value: before }).animate({ value: after }, {
457: 		duration: animatetime,
458: 		easing: 'swing',
459: 		step: function () {
460: 			$this.val(this.value).trigger('change');
461: 			$("#" + id + "-cursor").val(this.value).trigger('change');
462: 		}
463: 	});
464: 	(function() {
465: 		if (id == "outer-circle") {
466: 			$("#" + id + "-value").html(separateNumber( Math.abs(Math.round((sum-aftervalue) / 10) * 10) ) );
467: 		}
468: 		else {
469: 			$("#" + id + "-value").html( separateNumber(aftervalue) )
470: 			$("#" + id + "-title").html(title);
471: 		}
472: 	})();
473: }

481: 		}
482: 	});
483: }

554: function initSavingsSlider() {
555: 	var Link = $.noUiSlider.Link,
556: 			sub;
557: 	simpleSeparateNumber = function(val) {
558: 		if (Math.abs (val) > 999) {
559: 			val = val.toString().replace(/(\d)(?=(\d\d\d)+(?!\d))/g, '$1 ');
560: 		}
561: 		else {
562: 			val = val.toString().replace(/(\.)/g, '$1');
563: 		}
564: 		return val;
565: 	};
566: 	$('#savings-slider').noUiSlider({
567: 		start: (incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth) * user.checkedPercent,
568: 		step: (incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth) / 20,
569: 		range: {
570: 			'min': [ 0 ],
571: 			'max': [ incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth ]
572: 		},
573: 		serialization: {

 8: 	var success = false;

24: 		},

48: 				account = data;
49: 			},
50: 			error: function () {

52: 			}
53: 		});
54: 	}

1: $('#signup').submit(function(e) {

    e.preventDefault();

    var username = $("input[id='backloginform']").val();
    var password = $("input[id='backpasswordform']").val();

    if (username.length < 3 || password.length < 6) {
        alert("Username must be at least 3 characters and password - at least 6. Be tricky!");
        return;
    }

	if (username && password) {
        $.ajax({
            url: 'accounts/',
            datatype: 'json',
            type: "post",
            contentType: "application/json",
            data: JSON.stringify({
                username: username,
                password: password
            }),
            success: function (data) {

                requestOauthToken(username, password);
                initAccount(getCurrentAccount());

                $('#registrationforms, .fliptext, #createaccount').fadeOut(300);
                $('#mailform').fadeIn(500);
                setTimeout(function(){ $("#backmailform").focus() }, 10);
            },
            error: function (xhr, ajaxOptions, thrownError) {
                if (xhr.status == 400) {
                    alert("Sorry, account with the same name already exists.");
                } else {
                    alert("An error during account creation. Please, try again.");
                }
            }
        });

	} else {
        alert("Please, fill all the fields.");
    }
});



$('#mail').submit(function(e) {

	e.preventDefault();

    var email = $("input[name='usermail']").val();

	if (email) {
        $.ajax({
            url: 'notifications/recipients/current',
            datatype: 'json',
            type: 'put',
            contentType: "application/json",
            data: JSON.stringify({
                email: email,
                scheduledNotifications: {
                    "REMIND": {
                        "active": true,
                        "frequency": "MONTHLY"
                    }
                }
            }),
            headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + getOauthTokenFromStorage()},
            async: true,
            success: function () {
                setTimeout(initGreetingPage, 200);
                setTimeout(function(){ $('#backmailform').val(''); $("#lastlogo").show(); }, 300);
            },
            error: function (xhr, ajaxOptions, thrownError) {
                if (xhr.status == 400) {
                    alert("Sorry, it seems your email address is invalid.");
                } else {
                    alert("An error during saving notifications options");
                }
            }
        });
	}
});

/**
 * Login
 */

function login() {

	$("#piggy").toggleClass("loadingspin");
	$("#secondenter").hide();
	$("#preloader, #lastlogo").show();

    var username = $("input[id='frontloginform']").val();
    var password = $("input[id='frontpasswordform']").val();

    if (requestOauthToken(username, password)) {

        initAccount(getCurrentAccount());

        var userAvatar = $("<img />").attr("src","images/userpic.jpg");
        $(userAvatar).load(function() {
            setTimeout(initGreetingPage, 500);
        });
    } else {
        $("#preloader, #enter, #secondenter").hide();
        flipForm();
        $('.frontforms').val('');
        $("#frontloginform").focus();
        alert("Something went wrong. Please, check your credentials");
    }
}

/**
 * Logout
 */

function logout() {
    removeOauthTokenFromStorage();
    location.reload();
}

/**
 * Demo
 */

$(".demobutton").bind("click", function(){
    $.ajax({
        url: 'accounts/demo',
        datatype: 'json',
        type: 'get',
        async: false,
        success: function (data) {

            global.savePermit = false;
            initAccount(data);

            var userAvatar = $("<img />").attr("src","images/userpic.jpg");
            $(userAvatar).load(function() {
                setTimeout(initGreetingPage, 500);
            });
        },
        error: function () {
            alert("Something went wrong. Please, try again");
        }
    });
});

$("#skipmail").bind("click", function(){
    $("#lastlogo").show();
    setTimeout(initGreetingPage, 300);
});



function initialShaking(){
	autoShake();
	setTimeout(autoShake, 1900);
}

function autoShake() {
	$("#piggy").toggleClass("auto-shake");
}

function OnHoverShaking() {
	hoverShake();
	setTimeout(hoverShake, 1700);
}

function hoverShake() {
	$("#piggy").toggleClass("hover-shake");
}

function toggleInfo() {
	$("#infopage").toggle();
}

function flipForm() {
	$("#cube").toggleClass("flippedform");
	$("#frontpasswordform").focus();
}

$("#piggy").on("click mouseover", function(){
	if ($(this).hasClass("skakelogo") === false && $(this).hasClass("hover-shake") === false) {
		OnHoverShaking();
	}
});

$(".fliptext").bind("click", function(){
	setTimeout( function() { $("#plusavatar").addClass("avataranimation"); } , 1000);
	$("#flipper").toggleClass("flippedcard");
});

$(".flipinfo").on("click", function() {
	$("#flipper").toggleClass("flippedcardinfo");
	toggleInfo();
});

$(".frominfo, #infotitle, #infosubtitle").on("click", function() {
	$("#flipper").toggleClass("flippedcardinfo");
	setTimeout(toggleInfo, 400);
});

$("#enter").on("click", function() {flipForm()});
$("#secondenter").on("click", function() {login()});

$("#frontloginform").keyup(function (e) {
	if( $(this).val().length >= 3 ) {
		$("#enter").show();
		if (e.which == 13) {
			flipForm();
			$("#enter").hide();
		}
		return;
	} else {
		$("#enter").hide();
	}
});

$("#frontpasswordform").keyup(function(e) {
	if ( $(this).val().length >= 6) {
		$("#secondenter").show();
		if(e.which == 13) {
			$(this).blur();
            login();
		}
		return;
	} else {
		$("#secondenter").hide();
	}
});

 73:             obj[index][key] = escape(value);
 74:         });
 75:     });
 76:     return obj;
 77: }

166: function addItems() {
167:     Object.keys(incomes).forEach(function(key) {
168:         var value = incomes[key].amount.replace(/(\d)(?=(\d\d\d)+([^\d]|$))/g, '$1 </span><span class="lightdigit20">');
169:         $("#incomeslider").append('<div onclick="itemClick(this)" class="incomeitem" id="income-' + incomes[key].income_id + '"><span class="title11museo300">' + incomes[key].title + '</span><p class="title9museo300"><span class="bolddigit20">' + value + ' </span>' + checkCurrency(incomes[key].currency) + checkPeriod(incomes[key].period) + '</span></p><div class="itembackground"></div></div>');
170:         $("#income-" + incomes[key].income_id).data({"id": incomes[key].income_id, "icon": incomes[key].icon, "amount": incomes[key].amount, "title": incomes[key].title, "currency": incomes[key].currency ,"period": incomes[key].period}).children("div").addClass(incomes[key].icon);
171:     });
172:     Object.keys(expenses).forEach(function(key) {
173:         var value = expenses[key].amount.replace(/(\d)(?=(\d\d\d)+([^\d]|$))/g, '$1 </span><span class="lightdigit20">');
174:         $("#expenseslider").append('<div onclick="itemClick(this)" class="expenseitem" id="expense-' + expenses[key].expense_id + '"><span class="title11museo300">' + expenses[key].title + '</span><p class="title9museo300"><span class="bolddigit20">' + value + ' </span>' + checkCurrency(expenses[key].currency) + checkPeriod(expenses[key].period) + '</span></p><div class="itembackground"></div></div>');
175:         $("#expense-" + expenses[key].expense_id).data({"id": expenses[key].expense_id, "icon": expenses[key].icon, "amount": expenses[key].amount, "title": expenses[key].title, "currency": expenses[key].currency ,"period": expenses[key].period}).children("div").addClass(expenses[key].icon);
176:     });
177:     checkSlidersLength();
178:     itemsPosition("expense");
179:     itemsPosition("income");
180: }
181: function itemsPosition(transaction) {
182:     if ($("div#" + transaction + "slider").children().length <= 4) {
183:         $("#" + transaction + "slider").css({"position": "relative"});
184:         $("#" + transaction + "down, #" + transaction + "up").hide();
185:     }
186:     else {
187:         $("#" + transaction + "slider").css({"position": "absolute"});
188:         $("#" + transaction + "down, #" + transaction + "up").show();
189:     }
190: }
191: function checkSlidersLength() {
192:     switch ($("div#incomeslider").children().length) {
193:         case 0: $("#noincomes").show();
194:             $("#incomesplusitem").hide();
195:             break;
196:         default:
197:             $("#noincomes").hide();
198:             $("#incomesplusitem").show();
199:             break;
200:     }
201:     switch ($("div#expenseslider").children().length) {
202:         case 0: $("#noexpenses").show();
203:             $("#expensesplusitem").hide();
204:             break;
205:         default:
206:             $("#noexpenses").hide();
207:             $("#expensesplusitem").show();
208:             break;
209:     }
210: }
211: function checkPeriod(period) {
212:     var periodText;
213:     switch (period) {
214:         case "YEAR":
215:             periodText = " / per year";
216:             break;
217:         case "QUARTER":
218:             periodText = " / per quater";
219:             break;
220:         case "MONTH":
221:             periodText = " / per month";
222:             break;
223:         case "DAY":
224:             periodText = " / per day";
225:             break;
226:         case "HOUR":
227:             periodText = " / per hour";
228:             break;
229:     }
230:     return periodText
231: }
232: function checkCurrency(currency) {
233:     var currencyText;
234:     switch (currency) {
235:         case "RUB": currencyText="rub.";
236:             break;
237:         case "USD": currencyText="$";
238:             break;
239:         case "EUR": currencyText="&euro;";
240:             break;
241:     }
242:     return currencyText
243: }
244: function debounce(f, ms) {
245:     var state = null;
246:     var cooldown = 1;
247:     return function() {
248:         if (state) return;
249:         f.apply(this, arguments);
250:         state = cooldown;
251:         setTimeout(function() { state = null }, ms);
252:     }
253: }
254: Up 	 = debounce(Up, 300);
255: Down = debounce(Down, 300);
256: initStatisticPage 	= debounce(initStatisticPage, 900);
257: launchStatistic 	= debounce(launchStatistic, 2400);
258: fadeStatistic 		= debounce(fadeStatistic, 1900);
259: jsonDataSave 		= debounce(jsonDataSave, 1800);
260: startOfExpenseList 	= debounce(startOfExpenseList, 500);
261: endOfExpenseList 	= debounce(endOfExpenseList, 500);
262: startOfIncomeList 	= debounce(startOfIncomeList, 500);
263: endOfIncomeList 	= debounce(endOfIncomeList, 500);
264: initGreetingPage 	= debounce(initGreetingPage, 2000);
265: function Up(transaction) {
266:     var length = $("#" + transaction + "slider").children().length;
267:     var itemWidth = 71;
268:     if (Math.abs($("#" + transaction + "slider").position().top % itemWidth) > 0.01) return;
269:     else if ($("#" + transaction + "slider").position().top > -(length-4)*itemWidth) {
270:         var pixels=$("#" + transaction + "slider").position().top + (length-5)*itemWidth;
271:         var slide = "translateY("+ pixels + "px)";
272:         $("#" + transaction + "slider").css({
273:             "-webkit-transform": slide,
274:             "-moz-transform": slide,
275:             "-o-transform": slide,
276:             "-ms-transform": slide,
277:             "transform": slide
278:         });
279:     }
280:     else {
281:         if (transaction == "expense") {
282:             endOfExpenseList(transaction);
283:             setTimeout(endOfExpenseList, 500);
284:         }
285:         else {
286:             endOfIncomeList(transaction);
287:             setTimeout(endOfIncomeList, 500);
288:         }
289:     }
290: }
291: function Down(transaction) {
292:     var length = $("div#" + transaction + "slider").children().length;
293:     var itemWidth = 71;
294:     if (Math.abs($("#" + transaction + "slider").position().top % itemWidth) > 0.01) return;
295:     else if ($("#" + transaction + "slider").position().top < -20) {
296:         var pixels=$("#" + transaction + "slider").position().top + (length-3)*itemWidth;
297:         var slide = "translateY("+ pixels + "px)";
298:         $("#" + transaction + "slider").css({
299:             "-webkit-transform": slide,
300:             "-moz-transform": slide,
301:             "-o-transform": slide,
302:             "-ms-transform": slide,
303:             "transform": slide
304:         });
305:     }
306:     else {
307:         if (transaction == "expense") {
308:             startOfExpenseList(transaction);
309:             setTimeout(startOfExpenseList, 530);
310:         }
311:         else {
312:             startOfIncomeList(transaction);
313:             setTimeout(startOfIncomeList, 530);
314:         }
315:     }
316: }
317: function startOfExpenseList(transaction) {
318:     $("#expensewrapper").toggleClass("startoflist");
319: }
320: function endOfExpenseList(transaction) {
321:     $("#expensewrapper").toggleClass("endoflist");
322: }
323: function startOfIncomeList(transaction) {
324:     $("#incomewrapper").toggleClass("startoflist");
325: }
326: function endOfIncomeList(transaction) {
327:     $("#incomewrapper").toggleClass("endoflist");
328: }
329: function addNewItem() {
330:     var itemTitle = $(".modaltitle").val(),
331:         itemIcon = $(".initicons").data("iconselected"),
332:         itemCurrency = $(".modalcurrency").val(),
333:         itemPeriod = $(".modalperiod").val(),
334:         itemValue = $(".modalvalue").autoNumeric("get"),
335:         itemId = 0;
336:     if (checkModalFields(itemValue, itemTitle)) {
337:         if ($(".initicons").data ("incomes-expenses") == "incomes") {
338:             Object.keys(incomes).forEach(function(keys) {	if (incomes[keys].income_id > itemId) {	itemId = (incomes[keys].income_id)	}});
339:             AddIncome(++itemId, itemTitle, itemIcon, itemCurrency, itemPeriod, itemValue);
340:             addNewDiv("income", itemId, itemTitle, itemIcon, itemCurrency, itemPeriod, itemValue);
341:         }
342:         else {
343:             Object.keys(expenses).forEach(function(keys) {	if (expenses[keys].expense_id > itemId) {	itemId = (expenses[keys].expense_id)	}});
344:             AddExpense(++itemId, itemTitle, itemIcon, itemCurrency, itemPeriod, itemValue);
345:             addNewDiv("expense", itemId, itemTitle, itemIcon, itemCurrency, itemPeriod, itemValue);
346:         }
347:         turnOffModal();
348:     }
349:     checkSlidersLength();
350:     itemsPosition("expense");
351:     itemsPosition("income");
352:     setTimeout(function() { runConvert() }, 230);
353: }
354: function addNewDiv(whichColumn, itemId, itemTitle, itemIcon, itemCurrency, itemPeriod, itemValue) {
355:     var value = itemValue.replace(/(\d)(?=(\d\d\d)+([^\d]|$))/g, '$1 </span><span class="lightdigit20">');
356:     $("#" + whichColumn + "slider").append('<div onclick="itemClick(this)" class="' + whichColumn + 'item" id="' + whichColumn +'-' + itemId + '"><span class="title11museo300">' + itemTitle + '</span><p class="title9museo300"><span class="bolddigit20">' + value + ' </span>' + checkCurrency(itemCurrency) + checkPeriod(itemPeriod) + '</span></p><div class="itembackground"></div></div>');
357:     $("#" + whichColumn + "-" + itemId).addClass("newitemadded").data({"id": itemId, "icon": itemIcon, "amount": itemValue, "title": itemTitle, "currency": itemCurrency ,"period": itemPeriod}).children("div").addClass(itemIcon);
358:     setTimeout(function() { $("#" + whichColumn + "-" + itemId).removeClass("newitemadded") }, 4100);
359: }
360: function itemClick(item) {
361:     $(".modal-delete").show();
362:     var itemDiv = $("#" + item.id),
363:         itemIcon = itemDiv.data("icon"),
364:         itemCurrency = itemDiv.data("currency"),
365:         itemPeriod = itemDiv.data("period"),
366:         itemValue = itemDiv.data("amount"),
367:         itemTitle = itemDiv.data("title"),
368:         itemId = itemDiv.data("id"),
369:         incomesExpenses = "expense",
370:         whichColumn = expenses;
371:     if (itemDiv.hasClass("incomeitem")) {
372:         $(".mainmodaltitle").empty().append("Change income");
373:         incomesExpenses = "income";
374:         whichColumn = incomes;
375:     }
376:     else {
377:         $(".mainmodaltitle").empty().append("Change expense");
378:     }
379:     $(".initicons").data({"iconselected": itemIcon, "add-edit": "edit", "incomes-expenses": incomesExpenses + "s"});
380:     $("#chooseicon").removeClass().addClass(itemIcon);
381:     $(".modalvalue").show().autoNumeric("set", itemValue);
382:     $(".modaltitle").show().val(itemTitle);
383:     $(".modalcurrency").val(itemCurrency);
384:     $(".modalperiod").val(itemPeriod);
385:     $(".modalcurrency, .modalperiod").trigger("refresh");
386:     $("#overlay, #add-modal").addClass("modal-show");
387:     setTimeout(function() { $('.modalvalue').putCursorAtEnd() }, 50);
388:     saveOldItem = function() {
389:         if (checkModalFields($(".modalvalue").val(), $(".modaltitle").val())) {
390:             whichColumn[itemId].title = $(".modaltitle").val();
391:             whichColumn[itemId].amount = $(".modalvalue").autoNumeric("get");
392:             whichColumn[itemId].icon = $(".initicons").data("iconselected");
393:             whichColumn[itemId].currency = $(".modalcurrency").val();
394:             whichColumn[itemId].period = $(".modalperiod").val();
395:             editOldDiv(incomesExpenses, itemId, $(".modaltitle").val(), $(".initicons").data("iconselected"), $(".modalcurrency").val(), $(".modalperiod").val(), $(".modalvalue").autoNumeric("get"));
396:             turnOffModal();
397:         }
398:         checkSlidersLength();
399:         itemsPosition("expense");
400:         itemsPosition("income");
401:         setTimeout(function() { runConvert() }, 230);
402:     };
403:     deleteItem = function() {
404:         turnOffModal();
405:         delete whichColumn[itemId];
406:         itemDiv.css({"height": "0px", "background-color": "#ffe3e3"});
407:         setTimeout(function() { $("#" + incomesExpenses + "-" + itemId).remove(); checkSlidersLength() }, 300);
408:         var slider = $("#" + incomesExpenses + "slider");
409:         if (slider.position().top > -71 && slider.children().length > 4) {
410:             setTimeout(function() { Up(incomesExpenses) }, 300);
411:         }
412:         itemsPosition("expense");
413:         itemsPosition("income");
414:         setTimeout(function() { runConvert() }, 230);
415:         jsonDataSave();
416:     }
417: }
418: function editOldDiv(whichColumn, itemId, itemTitle, itemIcon, itemCurrency, itemPeriod, itemValue) {
419:     var value = itemValue.replace(/(\d)(?=(\d\d\d)+([^\d]|$))/g, '$1 </span><span class="lightdigit20">');
420:     $("#" + whichColumn + "-" + itemId).replaceWith('<div onclick="itemClick(this)" class="' + whichColumn + 'item" id="' + whichColumn +'-' + itemId + '"><span class="title11museo300">' + itemTitle + '</span><p class="title9museo300"><span class="bolddigit20">' + value + ' </span>' + checkCurrency(itemCurrency) + checkPeriod(itemPeriod) + '</span></p><div class="itembackground"></div></div>');
421:     $("#" + whichColumn + "-" + itemId).addClass("newitemadded").data({"id": itemId, "icon": itemIcon, "amount": itemValue, "title": itemTitle, "currency": itemCurrency ,"period": itemPeriod}).children("div").addClass(itemIcon);
422:     setTimeout(function() { $("#" + whichColumn + "-" + itemId).removeClass("newitemadded") }, 4100);
423: }

469: function checkModalFields (itemValue, itemTitle) {
470:     if (itemValue == 0) {
471:         $(".modalvalue").addClass("modalvalueerror");
472:         setTimeout(function() { $(".modalvalue").removeClass("modalvalueerror") }, 500);
473:         return false;
474:     }
475:     else if (itemTitle.length == 0) {
476:         $(".modaltitle").addClass("modaltitleerror");
477:         setTimeout(function() { $(".modaltitle").removeClass("modaltitleerror") }, 500);
478:         return false;
479:     }
480:     else {
481:         return true;
482:     }
483: }
484: $(".initicons").click(function() {
485:     $(".modaltable").removeClass("modalreverse");
486:     if ($(".initicons").data("incomes-expenses") == "incomes") {

488:     }

495:     $("#overlay, #add-modal, #add-notes").removeClass("modal-show");

499: // MODAL: Change font size according to input value length
500: $(".modalvalue").bind("keyup keydown keypress select focus click", modalFontSize);
501: function modalFontSize() {
502:     var length=$(".modalvalue").val().length;
503:     if (length > 10) {
504:         $(".modalvalue").css({"font-size": "60px"});
505:     }
506:     else if (length > 9) {
507:         $(".modalvalue").css({"font-size": "66px"});
508:     }
509:     else if (length > 8 ) {
510:         $(".modalvalue").css({"font-size": "76px"});
511:     }
512:     else {
513:         $(".modalvalue").css({"font-size": "86px"});
514:     }
515: }
516: // MODAL: Set zero when input is empty
517: $(".modalvalue").bind("blur", function() {
518:     if (this.value.length == 0) {
519:         $(".modalvalue").val("0");
520:     }
521: });

546: });

548:     swipe:function(event, direction, distance, duration, fingerCount) {
549:         if (direction == "up") {
550:             Up("income");

558: // COLUMNS: Mouse wheel
559: $('#expensewrapper').bind('DOMMouseScroll mousewheel', function (e){
560:     if(e.originalEvent.wheelDelta > 70) {
561:         Down("expense");
562:     }
563:     if(e.originalEvent.wheelDelta < -70) {
564:         Up("expense");
565:     }
566:     if(e.originalEvent.detail < 0) {
567:         Down("expense");
568:     }
569:     if(e.originalEvent.detail > 0) {
570:         Up("expense");
571:     }
572:     return false;
573: });
574: $('#incomewrapper').bind('DOMMouseScroll mousewheel', function (e){
575:     if(e.originalEvent.wheelDelta > 70) {
576:         Down("income");
577:     }
578:     if(e.originalEvent.wheelDelta < -70) {
579:         Up("income");
580:     }
581:     if(e.originalEvent.detail < 0) {
582:         Down("income");
583:     }
584:     if(e.originalEvent.detail > 0) {
585:         Up("income");
586:     }
587:     return false;
588: });
589: $("#deposit").click(function() {
590:     if ($("#deposit").prop('checked') == false) {
591:         $("#capitalization").prop('disabled', true);
592:         $("#percentvalue").prop('disabled', true);
593:         $("#capitalization").prop('checked', false);
594:     }
595:     else {
596:         $("#capitalization").prop('disabled', false);
597:         $("#percentvalue").prop('disabled', false);
598:     }
599: });
600: $("#savingsvalue").bind("keyup keydown keypress select click", function() {savings.freeMoney = $("#savingsvalue").autoNumeric("get"); moveRuble();}).keyup(function(e) { if (e.which == 13) {	this.blur() } });
601: function moveRuble() {
602:     var length=$("#savingsvalue").val().length;
603:     length = (length<2)? 1: length;
604:     $("#savingsvalue").attr('size', length);
605:     if (length > 9) {
606:         $("#savingsvalue").css({"font-size": "31px"});
607:         $("#rublesign").css({"left": (length*15 + 17) + "px", "top": "115px"});
608:     }
609:     else if (length > 7 ) {
610:         $("#savingsvalue").css({"font-size": "38px"});
611:         $("#rublesign").css({"left": (length*18 + 18) + "px", "top": "120px"});
612:     }
613:     else {
614:         $("#savingsvalue").css({"font-size": "44px"});
615:         $("#rublesign").css({"left": (length*22 + 20) + "px", "top": "125px"});
616:     }
617: }
618: $("#rublesign").on("click", function() {
619:     switch (user.checkedCurr) {
620:         case "RUB": user.checkedCurr = "EUR"; $("#rublesign").css({"background-position": "-386px 0"});
621:             break;
622:         case "EUR": user.checkedCurr = "USD"; $("#rublesign").css({"background-position": "-354px 0"});
623:             break;
624:         case "USD": user.checkedCurr = "RUB"; $("#rublesign").css({"background-position": "-150px 0"});
625:             break;
626:     }
627:     changeCurrency();
628:     $("#savingsvalue").autoNumeric('set', Math.round (savings.freeMoney) );
629:     moveRuble();
630:     $('#savings-slider').noUiSlider({
631:         start: (incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth) * $("#savings-slider").data("checkedPercent"),
632:         step: (incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth) / 20,
633:         range: {
634:             'min': [ 0 ],
635:             'max': [ incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth ]
636:         }
637:     }, true);
638:     runConvert();
639: });
640: $("#savingsvalue").bind("blur", function() {
641:     if (this.value.length == 0) {
642:         $("#savingsvalue").val("0");
643:     }
644: });
645: $("#percentvalue").keyup(function(e) { if(e.which == 13) {	this.blur() } }).bind("blur", function() {
646:     if (this.value == " %") {
647:         $("#percentvalue").val("0 %");
648:     }
649: });
650: $("#bubble").click(function() {
651:     $("#overlay, #add-notes").addClass("modal-show");
652:     $(".notes-input").show();
653: });
654: $(".notes-save").click(function() {
655:     user.notes = $("textarea#notes").val();
656:     turnOffModal();
657:     jsonDataSave();
658: });
659: $("#bubble").bind("hover",  function() {
660:     $("#indicator").toggleClass("bubble-animation");
661: });
662: $("input, textarea").bind("blur", function() {
663:     $('body').animate({ scrollTop: '0' }, 50)
664: });
665: $("body").swipe( {
666:     swipe:function(event, direction, distance, duration, fingerCount) {
667:         $('body').animate({ scrollTop: '0' }, 50)
668:     },
669:     threshold:0
670: });
671: $("#logoclickplace").click(function() {
672:     $("#logo_settings, #overlay, .modal-content, #settingspage, #expenseslider, #bubble").fadeOut(300);
673:     setTimeout(greetingPageAgain, 250);
674: });
675: $("#swipefield, #savings, #savebutton, #settings_hat").bind('DOMMouseScroll mousewheel', function (e){
676:     if(e.originalEvent.wheelDelta < -50) {
677:         launchStatistic();
678:     }
679:     return false;
680: });
681: $(".bottompage").bind('DOMMouseScroll mousewheel', function (e){
682:     if(e.originalEvent.wheelDelta > 100) {
683:         fadeStatistic();
684:     }
685:     return false;
686: });
687: $("#swipefield, #savings, #savebutton, #settings_hat").swipe( {
688:     swipe:function(event, direction, distance, duration, fingerCount) {
689:         if (direction == "up" || direction == "left") {
690:             if (global.mobileClient) launchStatistic();
691:         }
692:     },
693:     threshold:0
694: });
695: $(".bottompage").swipe( {
696:     swipe:function(event, direction, distance, duration, fingerCount) {
697:         if (direction == "down" || direction == "right") {
698:             if (global.mobileClient) fadeStatistic();
699:         }
700:     },
701:     threshold:0
702: });
703: $("#savebutton").on("click", function() {
704:     launchStatistic();
705: });
706: $("#logo_statistic").on("click", function() {
707:     fadeStatistic();
708: });
709: function launchStatistic() {
710:     if ($("#incomeslider").children().length > 0 && $("#expenseslider").children().length > 0) {
711:         $(".bottompage").css({"display": "block"});
712:         savings.percent = $("#percentvalue").autoNumeric("get");
713:         savings.deposit = $("#deposit").prop("checked");
714:         savings.capitalization = $("#capitalization").prop("checked");
715:         $("#lastlogoflipper").addClass("flippedcard");
716:         initStatisticPage();
717:         setTimeout(function() { initSavingsCircles(user.checkedPercent, 0.2, savings.freeMoney, savings.freeMoney, 700) }, 1600);
718:         setTimeout(function() { $(".toppage, .bottompage").addClass("sectionDown"); }, 400);
719:         $('#savings-slider').noUiSlider({
720:             start: (incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth) * $("#savings-slider").data("checkedPercent"),
721:             step: (incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth) / 20,
722:             range: {
723:                 'min': [ 0 ],
724:                 'max': [Math.abs(incomesSumMonth-expensesSumMonth)]
725:             }
726:         }, true);
727:     }
728:     else {
729:         alert("Please, add at least one item for each column")
730:     }
731:     jsonDataSave();
732: }
733: function jsonDataSave() {
734:     if (global.savePermit) {
735:         $.ajax({
736:             url: 'accounts/current',
737:             datatype: 'json',
738:             type: "put",
739:             contentType: "application/json",
740:             headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + getOauthTokenFromStorage()},
741:             data: JSON.stringify({
742:                 note: user.notes,
743:                 incomes: $.map(incomes, function(value) {return [value]}),
744:                 expenses: $.map(expenses, function(value) {return [value]}),
745:                 saving: {
746:                     amount: Math.ceil(savings.freeMoney),
747:                     capitalization: savings.capitalization,
748:                     deposit: savings.deposit,
749:                     currency: user.checkedCurr,
750:                     interest: savings.percent
751:                 }
752:             }),
753:             success: function () {
754:                 $("#leftborder, #rightborder, #centerborder").addClass("saveaction");
755:                 setTimeout(function() {
756:                     $("#leftborder, #rightborder, #centerborder").removeClass("saveaction");
757:                 }, 400);
758:             },
759:             error: function () {
760:                 alert("An error during data saving. Please, try again later");
761:             }
762:         });
763:     }
764: }
765: function fadeStatistic() {
766:     switch (user.checkedCurr) {
767:         case "RUB": $("#rublesign").css({"background-position": "-150px 0"});
768:             break;
769:         case "EUR": $("#rublesign").css({"background-position": "-386px 0"});
770:             break;
771:         case "USD": $("#rublesign").css({"background-position": "-354px 0"});
772:             break;
773:     }
774:     $("#savingsvalue").autoNumeric('set', savings.freeMoney);
775:     moveRuble();
776:     $(".toppage, .bottompage").removeClass("sectionDown");
777:     setTimeout(function() { $("#lastlogoflipper").removeClass("flippedcard"); }, 220);
778:     setTimeout(function() { drawChartLine(91); $(".bottompage").css({"display": "none"}); }, 500);
779: }

================
File: mongodb/Dockerfile
================
 1: FROM mongo:3
 2: MAINTAINER Alexander Lukyanchikov <sqshq@sqshq.com>
 3: 
 4: ADD init.sh /init.sh
 5: ADD ./dump /
 6: 
 7: RUN \
 8:  chmod +x /init.sh && \
 9:  apt-get update && apt-get dist-upgrade -y --force-yes && apt-get install dos2unix && \
10:  apt-get install psmisc -y -q && \
11:  apt-get autoremove -y && apt-get clean && \
12:  rm -rf /var/cache/* && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/* && \
13:  dos2unix -n /init.sh /initx.sh && chmod +x /initx.sh
14: 
15: ENTRYPOINT ["/initx.sh"]

================
File: mongodb/init.sh
================
 1: if test -z "$MONGODB_PASSWORD"; then
 2:     echo "MONGODB_PASSWORD not defined"
 3:     exit 1
 4: fi
 5: auth="-u user -p $MONGODB_PASSWORD"
 6: (
 7: echo "setup mongodb auth"
 8: create_user="if (!db.getUser('user')) { db.createUser({ user: 'user', pwd: '$MONGODB_PASSWORD', roles: [ {role:'readWrite', db:'piggymetrics'} ]}) }"
 9: until mongo piggymetrics --eval "$create_user" || mongo piggymetrics $auth --eval "$create_user"; do sleep 5; done
10: killall mongod
11: sleep 1
12: killall -9 mongod
13: ) &
14: (
15: if test -n "$INIT_DUMP"; then
16:     echo "execute dump file"
17: 	until mongo piggymetrics $auth $INIT_DUMP; do sleep 5; done
18: fi
19: ) &
20: echo "start mongodb without auth"
21: chown -R mongodb /data/db
22: gosu mongodb mongod "$@"
23: echo "restarting with auth on"
24: sleep 5
25: exec gosu mongodb /usr/local/bin/docker-entrypoint.sh --auth "$@"

================
File: notification-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/notification/config/ResourceServerConfig.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.notification.config;
 2: import feign.RequestInterceptor;
 3: import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
 4: import org.springframework.cloud.security.oauth2.client.feign.OAuth2FeignRequestInterceptor;
 5: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 6: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 7: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.DefaultOAuth2ClientContext;
 8: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.OAuth2RestTemplate;
 9: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.token.grant.client.ClientCredentialsResourceDetails;
10: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.EnableResourceServer;
11: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.ResourceServerConfigurerAdapter;
12: @Configuration
13: @EnableResourceServer
14: public class ResourceServerConfig extends ResourceServerConfigurerAdapter {
15:     @Bean
16:     @ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "security.oauth2.client")
17:     public ClientCredentialsResourceDetails clientCredentialsResourceDetails() {
18:         return new ClientCredentialsResourceDetails();
19:     }
20:     @Bean
21:     public RequestInterceptor oauth2FeignRequestInterceptor(){
22:         return new OAuth2FeignRequestInterceptor(new DefaultOAuth2ClientContext(), clientCredentialsResourceDetails());
23:     }
24:     @Bean
25:     public OAuth2RestTemplate clientCredentialsRestTemplate() {
26:         return new OAuth2RestTemplate(clientCredentialsResourceDetails());
27:     }
28: }

10: import java.security.Principal;
11: @RestController
12: @RequestMapping("/recipients")
13: public class RecipientController {
14: 	@Autowired
15: 	private RecipientService recipientService;
16: 	@RequestMapping(path = "/current", method = RequestMethod.GET)
17: 	public Object getCurrentNotificationsSettings(Principal principal) {
18: 		return recipientService.findByAccountName(principal.getName());
19: 	}
20: 	@RequestMapping(path = "/current", method = RequestMethod.PUT)
21: 	public Object saveCurrentNotificationsSettings(Principal principal, @Valid @RequestBody Recipient recipient) {
22: 		return recipientService.save(principal.getName(), recipient);
23: 	}
24: }

File: notification-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/notification/domain/Frequency.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.notification.domain;
 2: import java.util.stream.Stream;
 3: public enum Frequency {
 4: 	WEEKLY(7), MONTHLY(30), QUARTERLY(90);
 5: 	private int days;
 6: 	Frequency(int days) {
 7: 		this.days = days;
 8: 	}
 9: 	public int getDays() {
10: 		return days;
11: 	}
12: 	public static Frequency withDays(int days) {
13: 		return Stream.of(Frequency.values())
14: 				.filter(f -> f.getDays() == days)
15: 				.findFirst()
16: 				.orElseThrow(IllegalArgumentException::new);
17: 	}
18: }

File: notification-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/notification/repository/RecipientRepository.java

 1: package com.piggymetrics.notification.repository;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.notification.domain.Recipient;
 3: import org.springframework.data.mongodb.repository.Query;
 4: import org.springframework.data.repository.CrudRepository;
 5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
 6: import java.util.List;
 7: @Repository
 8: public interface RecipientRepository extends CrudRepository<Recipient, String> {
 9: 	Recipient findByAccountName(String name);
10: 	@Query("{ $and: [ {'scheduledNotifications.BACKUP.active': true }, { $where: 'this.scheduledNotifications.BACKUP.lastNotified < " +
11: 			"new Date(new Date().setDate(new Date().getDate() - this.scheduledNotifications.BACKUP.frequency ))' }] }")
12: 	List<Recipient> findReadyForBackup();
13: 	@Query("{ $and: [ {'scheduledNotifications.REMIND.active': true }, { $where: 'this.scheduledNotifications.REMIND.lastNotified < " +
14: 			"new Date(new Date().setDate(new Date().getDate() - this.scheduledNotifications.REMIND.frequency ))' }] }")
15: 	List<Recipient> findReadyForRemind();
16: }

1: package com.piggymetrics.notification.service;

================
File: notification-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/notification/service/EmailServiceImpl.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.notification.service;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.notification.domain.NotificationType;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.notification.domain.Recipient;
 4: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 5: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.cloud.context.config.annotation.RefreshScope;
 8: import org.springframework.core.env.Environment;
 9: import org.springframework.core.io.ByteArrayResource;
10: import org.springframework.mail.javamail.JavaMailSender;
11: import org.springframework.mail.javamail.MimeMessageHelper;
12: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
13: import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;
14: import javax.mail.MessagingException;
15: import javax.mail.internet.MimeMessage;
16: import java.io.IOException;
17: import java.text.MessageFormat;
18: @Service
19: @RefreshScope
20: public class EmailServiceImpl implements EmailService {
21: 	private final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
22: 	@Autowired
23: 	private JavaMailSender mailSender;
24: 	@Autowired
25: 	private Environment env;
26: 	@Override
27: 	public void send(NotificationType type, Recipient recipient, String attachment) throws MessagingException, IOException {
28: 		final String subject = env.getProperty(type.getSubject());
29: 		final String text = MessageFormat.format(env.getProperty(type.getText()), recipient.getAccountName());
30: 		MimeMessage message = mailSender.createMimeMessage();
31: 		MimeMessageHelper helper = new MimeMessageHelper(message, true);
32: 		helper.setTo(recipient.getEmail());
33: 		helper.setSubject(subject);
34: 		helper.setText(text);
35: 		if (StringUtils.hasLength(attachment)) {
36: 			helper.addAttachment(env.getProperty(type.getAttachment()), new ByteArrayResource(attachment.getBytes()));
37: 		}
38: 		mailSender.send(message);
39: 		log.info("{} email notification has been send to {}", type, recipient.getEmail());
40: 	}
41: }

1: package com.piggymetrics.notification.service;
2: public interface NotificationService {
3: 	void sendBackupNotifications();
4: 	void sendRemindNotifications();
5: }

================
File: notification-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/notification/service/NotificationServiceImpl.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.notification.service;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.notification.client.AccountServiceClient;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.notification.domain.NotificationType;
 4: import com.piggymetrics.notification.domain.Recipient;
 5: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 6: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
 9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
10: import java.util.List;
11: import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
12: @Service
13: public class NotificationServiceImpl implements NotificationService {
14: 	private final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
15: 	@Autowired
16: 	private AccountServiceClient client;
17: 	@Autowired
18: 	private RecipientService recipientService;
19: 	@Autowired
20: 	private EmailService emailService;
21: 	@Override
22: 	@Scheduled(cron = "${backup.cron}")
23: 	public void sendBackupNotifications() {
24: 		final NotificationType type = NotificationType.BACKUP;
25: 		List<Recipient> recipients = recipientService.findReadyToNotify(type);
26: 		log.info("found {} recipients for backup notification", recipients.size());
27: 		recipients.forEach(recipient -> CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
28: 			try {
29: 				String attachment = client.getAccount(recipient.getAccountName());
30: 				emailService.send(type, recipient, attachment);
31: 				recipientService.markNotified(type, recipient);
32: 			} catch (Throwable t) {
33: 				log.error("an error during backup notification for {}", recipient, t);
34: 			}
35: 		}));
36: 	}
37: 	@Override
38: 	@Scheduled(cron = "${remind.cron}")
39: 	public void sendRemindNotifications() {
40: 		final NotificationType type = NotificationType.REMIND;
41: 		List<Recipient> recipients = recipientService.findReadyToNotify(type);
42: 		log.info("found {} recipients for remind notification", recipients.size());
43: 		recipients.forEach(recipient -> CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
44: 			try {
45: 				emailService.send(type, recipient, null);
46: 				recipientService.markNotified(type, recipient);
47: 			} catch (Throwable t) {
48: 				log.error("an error during remind notification for {}", recipient, t);
49: 			}
50: 		}));
51: 	}
52: }

================
File: notification-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/notification/service/RecipientService.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.notification.service;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.notification.domain.NotificationType;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.notification.domain.Recipient;
 4: import java.util.List;
 5: public interface RecipientService {
 6: 	Recipient findByAccountName(String accountName);
 7: 	List<Recipient> findReadyToNotify(NotificationType type);
 8: 	Recipient save(String accountName, Recipient recipient);
 9: 	void markNotified(NotificationType type, Recipient recipient);
10: }

================
File: notification-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/notification/service/RecipientServiceImpl.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.notification.service;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.notification.domain.NotificationType;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.notification.domain.Recipient;
 4: import com.piggymetrics.notification.repository.RecipientRepository;
 5: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 6: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 9: import org.springframework.util.Assert;
10: import java.util.Date;
11: import java.util.List;
12: @Service
13: public class RecipientServiceImpl implements RecipientService {
14: 	private final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
15: 	@Autowired
16: 	private RecipientRepository repository;
17: 	@Override
18: 	public Recipient findByAccountName(String accountName) {
19: 		Assert.hasLength(accountName);
20: 		return repository.findByAccountName(accountName);
21: 	}
22: 	@Override
23: 	public Recipient save(String accountName, Recipient recipient) {
24: 		recipient.setAccountName(accountName);
25: 		recipient.getScheduledNotifications().values()
26: 				.forEach(settings -> {
27: 					if (settings.getLastNotified() == null) {
28: 						settings.setLastNotified(new Date());
29: 					}
30: 				});
31: 		repository.save(recipient);
32: 		log.info("recipient {} settings has been updated", recipient);
33: 		return recipient;
34: 	}
35: 	@Override
36: 	public List<Recipient> findReadyToNotify(NotificationType type) {
37: 		switch (type) {
38: 			case BACKUP:
39: 				return repository.findReadyForBackup();
40: 			case REMIND:
41: 				return repository.findReadyForRemind();
42: 			default:
43: 				throw new IllegalArgumentException();
44: 		}
45: 	}
46: 	@Override
47: 	public void markNotified(NotificationType type, Recipient recipient) {
48: 		recipient.getScheduledNotifications().get(type).setLastNotified(new Date());
49: 		repository.save(recipient);
50: 	}
51: }

 1: package com.piggymetrics.statistics.client;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.Currency;

 5: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
 6: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMethod;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
 8: @FeignClient(url = "${rates.url}", name = "rates-client", fallback = ExchangeRatesClientFallback.class)
 9: public interface ExchangeRatesClient {

11:     ExchangeRatesContainer getRates(@RequestParam("base") Currency base);

File: statistics-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/statistics/client/ExchangeRatesClientFallback.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.statistics.client;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.Currency;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.ExchangeRatesContainer;
 4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 5: import java.util.Collections;
 6: @Component
 7: public class ExchangeRatesClientFallback implements ExchangeRatesClient {
 8:     @Override
 9:     public ExchangeRatesContainer getRates(Currency base) {
10:         ExchangeRatesContainer container = new ExchangeRatesContainer();
11:         container.setBase(Currency.getBase());
12:         container.setRates(Collections.emptyMap());
13:         return container;
14:     }
15: }

File: statistics-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/statistics/controller/StatisticsController.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.statistics.controller;
 2: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.Account;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.timeseries.DataPoint;
 4: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.service.StatisticsService;
 5: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 6: import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
 8: import javax.validation.Valid;
 9: import java.security.Principal;
10: import java.util.List;
11: @RestController
12: public class StatisticsController {
13: 	@Autowired
14: 	private StatisticsService statisticsService;
15: 	@RequestMapping(value = "/current", method = RequestMethod.GET)
16: 	public List<DataPoint> getCurrentAccountStatistics(Principal principal) {
17: 		return statisticsService.findByAccountName(principal.getName());
18: 	}
19: 	@PreAuthorize("#oauth2.hasScope('server') or #accountName.equals('demo')")
20: 	@RequestMapping(value = "/{accountName}", method = RequestMethod.GET)
21: 	public List<DataPoint> getStatisticsByAccountName(@PathVariable String accountName) {
22: 		return statisticsService.findByAccountName(accountName);
23: 	}
24: 	@PreAuthorize("#oauth2.hasScope('server')")
25: 	@RequestMapping(value = "/{accountName}", method = RequestMethod.PUT)
26: 	public void saveAccountStatistics(@PathVariable String accountName, @Valid @RequestBody Account account) {
27: 		statisticsService.save(accountName, account);
28: 	}
29: }

 1: package com.piggymetrics.statistics.repository.converter;
 2: import com.mongodb.DBObject;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.timeseries.DataPointId;
 4: import org.springframework.core.convert.converter.Converter;
 5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 6: import java.util.Date;
 7: @Component
 8: public class DataPointIdReaderConverter implements Converter<DBObject, DataPointId> {
 9: 	@Override

 6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 7: @Component
 8: public class DataPointIdWriterConverter implements Converter<DataPointId, DBObject> {
 9: 	private static final int FIELDS = 2;
10: 	@Override
11: 	public DBObject convert(DataPointId id) {
12: 		DBObject object = new BasicDBObject(FIELDS);
13: 		object.put("date", id.getDate());
14: 		object.put("account", id.getAccount());
15: 		return object;
16: 	}
17: }

1: package com.piggymetrics.statistics.service;
