3: import java.math.BigDecimal;
4: import java.util.Map;
5: public interface ExchangeRatesService {
6: 	Map<Currency, BigDecimal> getCurrentRates();
7: 	BigDecimal convert(Currency from, Currency to, BigDecimal amount);
8: }

================
File: statistics-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/statistics/service/ExchangeRatesServiceImpl.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.statistics.service;
 2: import com.google.common.collect.ImmutableMap;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.client.ExchangeRatesClient;
 4: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.Currency;
 5: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.ExchangeRatesContainer;
 6: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 7: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 8: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
10: import org.springframework.util.Assert;
11: import java.math.BigDecimal;
12: import java.math.RoundingMode;
13: import java.time.LocalDate;
14: import java.util.Map;
15: @Service
16: public class ExchangeRatesServiceImpl implements ExchangeRatesService {
17: 	private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ExchangeRatesServiceImpl.class);
18: 	private ExchangeRatesContainer container;
19: 	@Autowired
20: 	private ExchangeRatesClient client;
21: 	@Override
22: 	public Map<Currency, BigDecimal> getCurrentRates() {
23: 		if (container == null || !container.getDate().equals(LocalDate.now())) {
24: 			container = client.getRates(Currency.getBase());
25: 			log.info("exchange rates has been updated: {}", container);
26: 		}
27: 		return ImmutableMap.of(
28: 				Currency.EUR, container.getRates().get(Currency.EUR.name()),
29: 				Currency.RUB, container.getRates().get(Currency.RUB.name()),
30: 				Currency.USD, BigDecimal.ONE
31: 		);
32: 	}
33: 	@Override
34: 	public BigDecimal convert(Currency from, Currency to, BigDecimal amount) {
35: 		Assert.notNull(amount);
36: 		Map<Currency, BigDecimal> rates = getCurrentRates();
37: 		BigDecimal ratio = rates.get(to).divide(rates.get(from), 4, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
38: 		return amount.multiply(ratio);
39: 	}
40: }

================
File: statistics-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/statistics/service/security/CustomUserInfoTokenServices.java
================
  1: package com.piggymetrics.statistics.service.security;
  2: import org.apache.commons.logging.Log;
  3: import org.apache.commons.logging.LogFactory;
  4: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.security.oauth2.resource.AuthoritiesExtractor;
  5: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.security.oauth2.resource.FixedAuthoritiesExtractor;
  6: import org.springframework.security.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken;
  7: import org.springframework.security.core.AuthenticationException;
  8: import org.springframework.security.core.GrantedAuthority;
  9: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.OAuth2RestOperations;
 10: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.OAuth2RestTemplate;
 11: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.resource.BaseOAuth2ProtectedResourceDetails;
 12: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.common.DefaultOAuth2AccessToken;
 13: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.common.OAuth2AccessToken;
 14: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.common.exceptions.InvalidTokenException;
 15: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.OAuth2Authentication;
 16: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.OAuth2Request;
 17: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.token.ResourceServerTokenServices;
 18: import java.util.*;
 19: public class CustomUserInfoTokenServices implements ResourceServerTokenServices {
 20: 	protected final Log logger = LogFactory.getLog(getClass());
 21: 	private static final String[] PRINCIPAL_KEYS = new String[] { "user", "username",
 22: 			"userid", "user_id", "login", "id", "name" };
 23: 	private final String userInfoEndpointUrl;
 24: 	private final String clientId;
 25: 	private OAuth2RestOperations restTemplate;
 26: 	private String tokenType = DefaultOAuth2AccessToken.BEARER_TYPE;
 27: 	private AuthoritiesExtractor authoritiesExtractor = new FixedAuthoritiesExtractor();
 28: 	public CustomUserInfoTokenServices(String userInfoEndpointUrl, String clientId) {
 29: 		this.userInfoEndpointUrl = userInfoEndpointUrl;
 30: 		this.clientId = clientId;
 31: 	}
 32: 	public void setTokenType(String tokenType) {
 33: 		this.tokenType = tokenType;
 34: 	}
 35: 	public void setRestTemplate(OAuth2RestOperations restTemplate) {
 36: 		this.restTemplate = restTemplate;
 37: 	}
 38: 	public void setAuthoritiesExtractor(AuthoritiesExtractor authoritiesExtractor) {
 39: 		this.authoritiesExtractor = authoritiesExtractor;
 40: 	}
 41: 	@Override
 42: 	public OAuth2Authentication loadAuthentication(String accessToken)
 43: 			throws AuthenticationException, InvalidTokenException {
 44: 		Map<String, Object> map = getMap(this.userInfoEndpointUrl, accessToken);
 45: 		if (map.containsKey("error")) {
 46: 			this.logger.debug("userinfo returned error: " + map.get("error"));
 47: 			throw new InvalidTokenException(accessToken);
 48: 		}
 49: 		return extractAuthentication(map);
 50: 	}
 51: 	private OAuth2Authentication extractAuthentication(Map<String, Object> map) {
 52: 		Object principal = getPrincipal(map);
 53: 		OAuth2Request request = getRequest(map);
 54: 		List<GrantedAuthority> authorities = this.authoritiesExtractor
 55: 				.extractAuthorities(map);
 56: 		UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken token = new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
 57: 				principal, "N/A", authorities);
 58: 		token.setDetails(map);
 59: 		return new OAuth2Authentication(request, token);
 60: 	}
 61: 	private Object getPrincipal(Map<String, Object> map) {
 62: 		for (String key : PRINCIPAL_KEYS) {
 63: 			if (map.containsKey(key)) {
 64: 				return map.get(key);
 65: 			}
 66: 		}
 67: 		return "unknown";
 68: 	}
 69: 	@SuppressWarnings({ "unchecked" })
 70: 	private OAuth2Request getRequest(Map<String, Object> map) {
 71: 		Map<String, Object> request = (Map<String, Object>) map.get("oauth2Request");
 72: 		String clientId = (String) request.get("clientId");
 73: 		Set<String> scope = new LinkedHashSet<>(request.containsKey("scope") ?
 74: 				(Collection<String>) request.get("scope") : Collections.<String>emptySet());
 75: 		return new OAuth2Request(null, clientId, null, true, new HashSet<>(scope),
 76: 				null, null, null, null);
 77: 	}
 78: 	@Override
 79: 	public OAuth2AccessToken readAccessToken(String accessToken) {
 80: 		throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Not supported: read access token");
 81: 	}
 82: 	@SuppressWarnings({ "unchecked" })
 83: 	private Map<String, Object> getMap(String path, String accessToken) {
 84: 		this.logger.debug("Getting user info from: " + path);
 85: 		try {
 86: 			OAuth2RestOperations restTemplate = this.restTemplate;
 87: 			if (restTemplate == null) {
 88: 				BaseOAuth2ProtectedResourceDetails resource = new BaseOAuth2ProtectedResourceDetails();
 89: 				resource.setClientId(this.clientId);
 90: 				restTemplate = new OAuth2RestTemplate(resource);
 91: 			}
 92: 			OAuth2AccessToken existingToken = restTemplate.getOAuth2ClientContext()
 93: 					.getAccessToken();
 94: 			if (existingToken == null || !accessToken.equals(existingToken.getValue())) {
 95: 				DefaultOAuth2AccessToken token = new DefaultOAuth2AccessToken(
 96: 						accessToken);
 97: 				token.setTokenType(this.tokenType);
 98: 				restTemplate.getOAuth2ClientContext().setAccessToken(token);
 99: 			}
100: 			return restTemplate.getForEntity(path, Map.class).getBody();
101: 		}
102: 		catch (Exception ex) {
103: 			this.logger.info("Could not fetch user details: " + ex.getClass() + ", "
104: 					+ ex.getMessage());
105: 			return Collections.<String, Object>singletonMap("error",
106: 					"Could not fetch user details");
107: 		}
108: 	}
109: }

================
File: statistics-service/src/main/java/com/piggymetrics/statistics/service/StatisticsServiceImpl.java
================
 1: package com.piggymetrics.statistics.service;
 2: import com.google.common.collect.ImmutableMap;
 3: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.*;
 4: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.timeseries.DataPoint;
 5: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.timeseries.DataPointId;
 6: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.timeseries.ItemMetric;
 7: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.domain.timeseries.StatisticMetric;
 8: import com.piggymetrics.statistics.repository.DataPointRepository;
 9: import org.slf4j.Logger;
10: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
11: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
12: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
13: import org.springframework.util.Assert;
14: import java.math.BigDecimal;
15: import java.math.RoundingMode;
16: import java.time.Instant;
17: import java.time.LocalDate;
18: import java.time.ZoneId;
19: import java.util.Date;
20: import java.util.List;
21: import java.util.Map;
22: import java.util.Set;
23: import java.util.stream.Collectors;
24: @Service
25: public class StatisticsServiceImpl implements StatisticsService {
26: 	private final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass());
27: 	@Autowired
28: 	private DataPointRepository repository;
29: 	@Autowired
30: 	private ExchangeRatesService ratesService;
31: 	@Override
32: 	public List<DataPoint> findByAccountName(String accountName) {
33: 		Assert.hasLength(accountName);
34: 		return repository.findByIdAccount(accountName);
35: 	}
36: 	@Override
37: 	public DataPoint save(String accountName, Account account) {
38: 		Instant instant = LocalDate.now().atStartOfDay()
39: 				.atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toInstant();
40: 		DataPointId pointId = new DataPointId(accountName, Date.from(instant));
41: 		Set<ItemMetric> incomes = account.getIncomes().stream()
42: 				.map(this::createItemMetric)
43: 				.collect(Collectors.toSet());
44: 		Set<ItemMetric> expenses = account.getExpenses().stream()
45: 				.map(this::createItemMetric)
46: 				.collect(Collectors.toSet());
47: 		Map<StatisticMetric, BigDecimal> statistics = createStatisticMetrics(incomes, expenses, account.getSaving());
48: 		DataPoint dataPoint = new DataPoint();
49: 		dataPoint.setId(pointId);
50: 		dataPoint.setIncomes(incomes);
51: 		dataPoint.setExpenses(expenses);
52: 		dataPoint.setStatistics(statistics);
53: 		dataPoint.setRates(ratesService.getCurrentRates());
54: 		log.debug("new datapoint has been created: {}", pointId);
55: 		return repository.save(dataPoint);
56: 	}
57: 	private Map<StatisticMetric, BigDecimal> createStatisticMetrics(Set<ItemMetric> incomes, Set<ItemMetric> expenses, Saving saving) {
58: 		BigDecimal savingAmount = ratesService.convert(saving.getCurrency(), Currency.getBase(), saving.getAmount());
59: 		BigDecimal expensesAmount = expenses.stream()
60: 				.map(ItemMetric::getAmount)
61: 				.reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
62: 		BigDecimal incomesAmount = incomes.stream()
63: 				.map(ItemMetric::getAmount)
64: 				.reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
65: 		return ImmutableMap.of(
66: 				StatisticMetric.EXPENSES_AMOUNT, expensesAmount,
67: 				StatisticMetric.INCOMES_AMOUNT, incomesAmount,
68: 				StatisticMetric.SAVING_AMOUNT, savingAmount
69: 		);
70: 	}
71: 	private ItemMetric createItemMetric(Item item) {
72: 		BigDecimal amount = ratesService
73: 				.convert(item.getCurrency(), Currency.getBase(), item.getAmount())
74: 				.divide(item.getPeriod().getBaseRatio(), 4, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
75: 		return new ItemMetric(item.getTitle(), amount);
76: 	}
77: }
